1b1('dul ba): @#//rgya gar skad du/bi na ya ba stu/bod skad du/'dul ba gzhi/bam po dang po//dkon 
1b2('dul ba): mchog gsum la phyag 'tshal lo//gang gis 'ching rnams yang dag rab bcad cing //mu stegs tshogs rnams 
1b3('dul ba): thams cad rab bcom ste//sde dang bcas pa'i bdud rnams nges bcom nas//byang chub 'di brnyes de la phyag 
1b4('dul ba): 'tshal lo//khyim don che chung spangs te dang po rab 'byung dka'//rab 'byung thob nas yul spyad dag gis dga' thob 
1b5('dul ba): dka'//mngon dga' ji bzhin don bskyed yang dag byed pa dka'//dur smrig gos 'chang mkhas pa tshul las nyams pa 
1b6('dul ba): dka'//gzhi rnams kyi spyi sdom la/rab 'byung gso sbyong gzhi dang ni//dgag dbye dbyar dang ko lpags gzhi//sman dang 
1b7('dul ba): gos dang /sra brgyad dang //ko sham bi dang las kyi gzhi//dmar ser can dang gang zag dang //spo dang gso sbyor gzhag pa dang //
1b8('dul ba): gnas mal dang ni rtsod pa dang //dge 'dun dben gnas bsdus pa yin//rab tu byung ba'i spyi sdom la/shA ri'i bu 
2a1('dul ba): @#//dang mu stegs can//dge tshul gnyis dang bya rog skrod//dgra bcom bsad dang lag rdum gyi//sde tshan yang dag bsdus pa yin//sdom la shA ri'i bu dang rab 'byung dang //brnyen par 
2a2('dul ba): rdzogs par gnang ba dang //nye sdos tshogs ni bsdus pa dang //lnga pa'i sde tshan bsdus pa yin//byang chub sems dpa' dga' ldan gyi gnas na bzhugs pa na//yul ad ga dag na ang ga'i 
2a3('dul ba): rgyal po zhes bya bas rgyal srid 'byor ba/rgyas pa bde ba lo legs pa skye bo dang mi mang pos gang ba byed du bcug go/res 'ga' ni ad gi'i rgyal po dpung dang mthu che ba yin la/res 'ga' ni rgyal po pad ma chen po zhes bya bas rgyal sring 'byor 
2a4('dul ba): ba rgyas pa bde ba lo legs ba skye bo dang mi mang pos gad ba byed du bcug go/re sa 'ga' ni ad gi'i rgyal bo dpung dang mthu che bayin la/res 'ga' ni rnam pa dang mthu che ba yin no//gang gi 
2a5('dul ba): tshe ad ga'i rgyal po dpung dang mthuche bde'i tshe na/des dpung gi tshegs yan lag bzhi pa/glang po che pa'i tshogs dang /rta pa'i tshogs dang /shing rta pa'i tshogs dang /dpung bu chung gi tshogs go bskon te/
2a6('dul ba): yul ma ga d+hA rgyal po'i khab ma gtogs pa bcom nas phyir ldog par phyed do//gang gi tshe rgyal po pad ma chen po dpung dang mthu che pa de'i tshe na/des kyang dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi ba glang po che pa'i tshogs dang
2a7('dul ba): rta pa'i tshogs dang /shing brta pa'i tshogs dang /dpang bu chung gi tshogs go bskon te/yul ang ga tsam pa ma gtogs pa bcom nas phyer ldog par byed do//de nas dus gzhan zhig na ad ga'i rgyal po 
2a8('dul ba): dpung dang mthu che bar gyur nas /des dpung gi tshogs yan la ga bzhi pa glang po che pa'i tshogs da nga /rta pa'i tshogs da nga /shing rta pa' tshogs da nga /dpang bu chung gi tsho gas go bskon te yul ma ga d+ha gzhom
2b1('dul ba): par brtsams pa dang/ma ga d+ha na gnas pa'i skye po'i tshogs kyis rgyal po pad ma chen po la lha ang gi' rgyal pos dbung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi pa glang po che pa'i tshogs dang/rta pa'i tshogs dang/
2b2('dul ba): shing rta pa'i tshogs dang/dpung bu chung gi tshogs go bskon te yul ma ga d+ha 'joms par bgyid do zhes sbron pa dag btang ngo//rgyal po pad ma chen pos kyang thos nas dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi
2b3('dul ba): pa glang po che pa'i tshogs dad/rta pa'i tshogs dang/shing rta pa'i tshogs dang//dpung bu chung gi tshogs ga bskon te ang ga'i rgyal po dang g.yul sprad pa'i phyir 'phags so//de nas ang ga'i rgyal
2b4('dul ba): pos rgyal po pad ma chen pa'i glang po che'i tshogs thams cad phrogs shing/rta ba'i tshogs dang/shing rta ba'i tshogs dang/dbung bu chung gi tshogs thams cad kyang phrogs so//rgyal po bad ma chen po yang
2b5('dul ba): pham pa dang/skrag pa dang/bcom pa dang/gzhan rgyal ba dang/rgyab kyis phyogs par byas te/rgyal po'i khab tu zhugs nas sgo bcad de/rab dag gi khar sprengs nas 'dug go/ang ga'i rgyal pos
2b6('dul ba): rgyal po bad ma chen po la pho nya btang ste/gal te phyir 'byung na de lta na legs/gal te phyir mi 'byung na ji ste steng gi nam mkha' la 'gro na ni mda' brgyud pas khyed ltung bar bya'o//ji ste sa'i 'og tu 'gro
2b7('dul ba): na ni 'chil pa dang drad pa'i tshul gyis drad par bya'o//ji ste ra'i rtse mor 'jog na ni der yang khyed thar pa med do zhes sbrid do//rgyal po pad ma chen pos phrin yig blags nas de mi bde bar gyur te/lag pa la
2b8('dul ba): 'gram pa gtad nas sems khongs su chud cing 'dug 'dug nas des blon po rnams la smras pa/shes ldan dag ang ga'i rgyal po 'di ni gdug pa/pha rol gnon pa/dbung dang mthu che bar gyur la/des
3a1('dul ba): @#//yu bu cag la 'di dang 'di skad ces spring na/de la yu bu cag gis ji ltar bsgrub par bya/de dag gis tshigs su bcad pa smras pa/yul dang srog la gnod gyur na//skyes bus kun tu 
3a2('dul ba): srog bsrung bya//blo yis gnyis ka dpyad byas na//yul ni yang rnyed srog rnams min//lha rnam pa thams cad du slar 'byung bar bgyi'o//de ral gri mgul du thogs te phyir byung ba dang /ang gi'i rgyal pos 
3a3('dul ba): de la lo thang dang bca' phab ste de nyid du bzhag go/gang gi tshe bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' dga' ldan gyi gnas na bzhugs pas gzigs pa lnga po dag la gzigs nas 'dod pa na spyod pa'i lha drug po 
3a4('dul ba): dag la lan gsum bsgo ba mdzad de/glang po che ltar snang bar bsgyur te/yum gyi lhums su zhugs pa de'i tshe nas chen po shin tu g.yos par gyur cing 'jig rten 'di thams cad kyang sum bcu rtsa gsum pa'i lha 
3a5('dul ba): rnams kyi kha dog gi ma thubs lhag pa'i snang ba rgya chen pos khyab par gyur cing /'jig rten gyi 'jig rten gyi bar gang na nyi ma dang zla ba 'di ltar rdzu 'phrul che ba/'di ltar mthu che ba 'di gnyis kyi 'od dag nyams su mi myong ba'i 
3a6('dul ba): mun pa mun gnag mun par byed pas/gnag par gyur pa gang dag yin pa de dag kyang de'i tshe na snang ba rgya chen pos khyab bar gyur nas/sems can gang dag der skyes ba dag gis rang gi lag pa brkyang ba yang mi 
3a7('dul ba): mthong ba de dag gis kyang 'od des sems can gcig gis gcig mthong nas shes ldan dag sems can gzhan yang 'dir skyes so//shes ldan dag sems can bzhan yang 'dir skyes so zhes shes par 
3a8('dul ba): gyur to//gang gi tshe bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' bltam pa de'i tshe na grong khyer chen po bzhi po dag tu rgyal po chen po bzhi dag gi bu yang btsas par gyur te/rgyal po'i khab tu rgyal po pad ma chen 
3b1('dul ba): chen po'i bu btsas so//mnyan yod du ni rgyal po rtsibs kyis 'phur tshangs byin gyi bu btsas so//'phags rgyal du ni rgyal po mu khyud mtha' yas kyi bu btsas so//kau sham bir 
3b2('dul ba): ni rgyal po dmag brgya pa'i bu btsas so//bcom ldan 'das byang chub sems dpa' bltam pa na 'jig rten thams cad sum bcu rtsa gsum pa'i lha rnams kyi kha dog gi mthu bas lhag pa'i snang ba rgya chen pos 
3b3('dul ba): khyab par gyur cing 'jig rten gyi 'jig rten gyi bar gang na nyi ma dang zla ba 'di ltar rdzu 'phrul che ba/'di ltar mthu che ba 'di gnyis kyi 'di dag nyams su mi myong ba'i mun pa mun gnag mun par byed pas gnag par gyur 
3b4('dul ba): pa gang dag yin pa de dag kyang de'i tshe na snang ba rgya chen pos khyab par gyur nas/sems can gang dag der skyes pa dag gis rang gi lag pa brkyang ba yang mi mthong ba de dag gis kyang 'od des sems can 
3b5('dul ba): gcig gis gcig mthong nas shes ldan dag sems can gzhan yang 'dir skyes so//shes ldan dag sems can gzan yang 'dir skyes so/zhes shes par gyur to//rgyal po pad ma chen pos bsams pa 
3b6('dul ba): bdag gi bu btsas pa na 'jig rten nyi ma'i gzugs shar ba ltar snang zhing 'di btsun mo gzugs can gyi bu yin pas gzhon nu'i de'i ming gzugs can snying por gdags so snyam nas bu de'i ming gzugs can snying
3b7('dul ba): po zhes bya bar btags so//rgyal po rtsibs kyis 'phur tshangs byin gyis bsams pa/bdag gi bu btsas pa na 'jug rten gsal ba gsal ba ltar snang las gzhon nu 'di'i ming gsal rgyal du gdags 
3b8('dul ba): so snyam nas bu de'i ming gsal rgyal zhes bya bar btags so/rgyal po dmag brgya pas bsams pa/bdag gi bur btsas pa na 'jig rten nya ma shar ba ltar snang bar gyur bas gzhon nu 'di'i ming shar 
4a1('dul ba): //par gdags so snyam nas bu de'i ming shar pa zhes bya bar btags so//rgyal po mu khyud mtha' yas kyis bsams pa/bdag gi bu btsas pa na 'jig rten rab tu snang bas snang bar gyur pas gzhon nu 'di'i ming rab
4a2('dul ba): snang zhes bya bar gdags so snyam nas bu ngo'i ming rab snang zhes bya bar btags so//rgyal po re res bsams pa yang bdag gi bu 'di'i mthu yin no//bdag gi bu 'di'i mthu yin//snyam mod kyi/
4a3('dul ba): de ni de lta ma yin te/'di ltar de dag thams cad ni byang chub sems dpa'i mthu yin pa'i phyir ro//gang gi nyin mo gzhon nu gzugs can snying po btsas pa de nyid kyi nyin mo blon po lnga brgya dgag bu lnga brgya
4a4('dul ba): yang btsas te/de dag kyang rigs dang 'thun par ming btags so//gzhon nu gzugs can snyingapo ma ma brgyad po pang na 'tshe ba'i ma ma gnyis dang /nu ma snun pa'i ma ma gnyis dang /dri ma 'phyi ba'i ma
4a5('dul ba): ma gnyis dang /rtsed grogs kyi ma ma gnyis la brjes su gtang ngo//de ma ma brgyad po dag gis 'o ma dang /zho dang mar dang /zhun mar dang /mar gyi snying khu dang /gzhan yang yo byad kyi bye brag gtso
4a6('dul ba): bo gtso bo dag gis srid par byed /skyed bar byed cing rdzing na gnas pa'i bad ma bzhin myur tu skyed par byed do//de gang gi tshe chen por gyur pa de'i tshe yi ge dang /grangs dang /rtsis dang /lag rtsis
4a7('dul ba): dang /dbyung ba dang //gzhug pa dang /gzhag pa la nye bar zhugs nas/de yi ge'i mthar phyin 'byed pa dang /klog pa dang /mkhas pa dang /spyod pa gsal ba dang /brtag pa brgyad po
4a8('dul ba): 'di lta ste/rin po che brtag pa dang /gzhi brtag pa dang /gos brtag pa dang /shing rta brtag pa dang /glang po che brtag pa dang /rta brtag pa dang /bud med brtag pa dang /skyes pa brtag pa la
4b1('dul ba): mkhas par gyur to//rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bor dbang skur ba ljongs kyi dbang phyug mthu dang brtson 'grus thob pa/sa chen po'i dkyil 'khor mngon par rgyal bas byas
4b2('dul ba): te gnas pa rnams kyi bzo'i gnas dang /las kyi gnas tha dad par gyur pa gang dag yin pa 'di lta ste/glang po che'i gnyer bzhon pa dang /rta bzhon pa dang /shing rta'i thabs dang /ral gri'i thabs 
4b3('dul ba): dang /'phod dang /phyir bsnung pa dang /mdun du bsnur ba dang /lcags kyis bsgyur thabs dang /zhags pa gdab pa dang /mda' bo che 'phen thabs dang /'dzin stangs dang /gom stangs dang /thor 
4b4('dul ba): tshugs dang /gcad pa dang /dral ba dang /dbug pa dang /gnas lnga po 'di lta ste/rgyang nas 'phog pa dang /sgra grag bar 'phog pa dang /gnad du 'phog pa dang /mi 'tshor bar 'phog pa dang /
4b5('dul ba): tsha bas tshe ba de dag la yang zhugs shing byed par gyur to//blon po'i bu lnga brgya po de dag kyang bzo'i gnas dang /lasakyi gnas ngag la byang bar gyur to/de phas bzo sbyang ba bco brgyad po dag la 
4b6('dul ba): bcug nas de'i ming bzo sbyangs gzugs can snying po/bzo sbyangs gzugs can nyid po zhes bya bar gyur to//de nas dus gzhan zhig na gzhon nu gzugs can snying po glang po che la zhon te ljongs su 
4b7('dul ba): byung ba dang /des ang ga'i rgyal po'i zho shas 'tsho ba dag lo thang dang dpya sdud pa mthong nas/des smras pa/shes ldan dag lo thang dang /dpya sdud ba 'di dag su'i yin/de dag gis smras pa/
4b8('dul ba): lha ang ga'i rgyal po'i lags so/des smras pa/ci yu bu cag de la lo thang 'bul ba yin nam/de dag gis smras pa/lha lo thang 'bul ba lags so//des smras pa/shes ldan dang /zho shes 'tsho ba 
5a1('dul ba): @#//de dag bos shig/de dag gis bos pa dang /des smras pa/shes ldan dag de yang rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bor dbang bskur ba yin la/nged kyang rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi por dbang bskur ba yin na/ci'i phyir 
5a2('dul ba): khyed cag lo thang dang dpya sdud par byed/deng la phyin ched lo thang dang dpya ma sdud cig/de dag gis bsams pa/gzhon nu 'di ni rgyus mi shes pa yin pas bdag cag rgyal po pad ma chen po'i gan du 'dod zhing son 
5a3('dul ba): nas bsnyad par bya'o snyam nas/de dag rgyal po pad ma chen po 'i gan du song ste phyin nas smras pa/lha bdag cag ad ga'i rgyal po'i zho shas 'tsho ba lo thang dang dpya sdud ba na gzhon nu gzugs can snying pos bgag na/
5a4('dul ba): ci bsdu bar 'tshal lam/'on te slar ma che bar 'tshal/des smras pa/shes ldan ngag gzhon nu de ni rgyus mi shes pa yin bas khyed cag sngon ji ltar lo thang dang dpya sdud bde bzhin du bsdus shig/de dag gis 
5a5('dul ba): yang bsdu bar brtsams te/de dag 'ongs nas phyir ldog pa gzhon nu gzugs can nyid pos mthong nas smras pa/shes ldan dag ngas khyed la phyin chad lo thang dang dpya ma sdung cig ces ma bzlog gam/ci'i phyir yang 
5a6('dul ba): khyed lo thang dang dpya sdud/gal te re shig 'khod na de ltar na legs/gal te mi 'khod na mtshams chen po gcad par bya'o//de dag skrag nas bsams pa/gzhon nu 'di ni gdug pa pha rol gnon pa yin bas 
5a7('dul ba): bdag cag la gnod pa byed par 'gyur ba gang yin pa de lta bu'i gnas yod par 'gyur gyis/bdag cag ang ga'i rgyal po'i gan du dong ste sbran bar bya'o snyam nas de dag ang ga'i rgyal po'i gan tu dong ste phyin nas smras 
5a8('dul ba): pa/lha bdag cag lha'i zho shas 'tsho ba lags te/bdag cag lha'i lo thang dang dpya sdud pa na rgyal po pad ma chen po'i bu gzhon nu gzugs can snying po des bzlog lags na/dgal te lhas btang snyoms mdzad na 
5b1('dul ba): slad kyis gnod pa chen po bgyid par 'gyur ba gang lags pa de lta bu'i gnas mchis par gyur ro//tshigs su bcad pa yang smras pa/ji srid shing ni gzhon pa'i tshe//sen mo dag gis 
5b2('dul ba): bcad nus pa//de nyid chen por skyes pa na//sta re ba rgyu ba kyang bcad par dka'//ang ga'i rgyal pos rgyal po bang ma chen po la gzhon nu gzugs can snying po mgul ba nas thogs la chur bkri ba 'am/yang 
5b3('dul ba): na rtswa dang chas sta gon gyis shig dang /nga nyid 'o nir 'ong ngo zhes phrin yig sprid do//rgyal po pad ma chen pos phrin yig blags nas de mi bde bar gyur te lag pa 'gram pa la gtad nas sems khongs su chud 
5b4('dul ba): cing 'dug 'dug nas/des gzhon nu gzugs can nyid po pos nas smras pa/ci'i phyir khyod kyis ang ga'i rgyal po'i zho shas 'tsho ba dag lo thang dang dpya sdud bzlog/des khros te nga la 'di dang 'di skad ces sprid do//des 
5b5('dul ba): smras pa/lha ci skol de la lo thang 'bul ba lags sam/bu lo thang 'bul ba yin no//des smras pa/lha de yang rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bor dbang bskur ba la gsal/skol kyang rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi 
5b6('dul ba): par dbang bskur ba lags na/ci'i slad du skol de la lo thang dang dpya 'bul/lhas bdag la dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi ba stsal pa 'ba' zhig mdzod gcig dang /bdag gis ang ga'i rgyal po dang lhan cig g.yul 
5b7('dul ba): ngor g.yul sprad par bgyi'o//de nas rgyal po pad ma chen pos ang ga'i rgyal po la khyod kyi mthu dang /dpung dang /brtson 'grus dang /pha rol gnon pa gang dag yin pa de dag thams cad mnyams par gyis shig 
5b8('dul ba): ces phrin yig spring ngo //ang ga'i rgyal pos thos nas mi bzod pa skyes te blo na bo rnams la smras pa/shes ldan dag de'i yul 'khor gzhom par byas/dpung gi tshogs yan lag pa zhi ba go skon cig/
6a1('dul ba): @#//des dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi pa glang po che ba'i tshogs dang /rta pa'i tshogs dang /shing rta pa'i tshogs dang /dpung bu chung gi tshogs go bskon te yul ma gang+ga gzhom par brtsams pa dang /ma gang+ga 
6a2('dul ba): na gnas pa'i skye bo'i tshogs kyis rgyal po pad ma chen po la lha ang gi'i rgyal po 'di dpung gi tshogs yan lagabazhi pa glang po che ba'i tsho gas dang /rta pa'i tshogs/shing rta pa'i tshogs dang dpung bu chung 
6a3('dul ba): gi tshogs go bskon te yulam ga d+ha 'joms par bgyid do zhes sbron pa dag btang ngo //rgyal po pad ma chen pos thos nas de mi bde bar gyur te lag pa 'gram pa la gtad nas sems khongs su chud cing 'dug 
6a4('dul ba): 'dug nas/des gzhon nu gzugs can snying po bos te/de la dpung gi tshogs yan la ga bzhi pa byin no//gzhon nu gzugs can snyingapos blon po'i bu lnga brgya bsdus te smras pa/shes ldan dag 
6a5('dul ba): da ni ang ga'i rgyal po dang lhan cig g.yul dor g.yul sprod par byed na shes ldan dag ci byed/de dag gis smras pa/gzhon nu khyod kyi gnas skabs gang lags pa de bdag cag gi yang lags so//
6a6('dul ba): des tshigs su bcad pa smras pa/mi gang la ni khyim dpal gnas//de ni kun gyis yongs bsrung bya//de nyams na ni kun nyams te//lte ba chag pas rtsibs rnams bzhin//nga khyod kyis kun nas 
6a7('dul ba): yongs su bsrung bar bya'o//de dag gis smras pa/lha'i gnas skabs gang lags pa/de bdag cag gi yang lag la/lha'i zhabs gdan pa de na bdag cag gi mgo mchis so//de nas des dpung gi tshogs 
6a8('dul ba): yan lag bzhi pa go bskon te/rgyal po'i khab nas byung ngo //rgyal po pad ma chen po steng gi khang bzangs kyi gzhir gtogs pa na blon po'i tshogs kyis bskor cing 'dug pa des de byung ba mthong nas 
6b1('dul ba): des blon po rnams la smras pa/shes ldan dag dmag 'di su'i yin/de dag gis smras pa/lha gzhon nu gzugs can snying po'i legs so//des smras pa/shes ldan dag e ma 'di dmag mang po dang 
6b2('dul ba): ldan no//zhes smras pas/de'i ming dmag ldan gzugs can snying po//dmag ldan gzugs can snyingapo zhes bya bar gyur te//de na kha cig gis ni bzo sbyangs gzugs can snying po zhes bya bar shes so//kha cig 
6b3('dul ba): gis ni dmag ldan gzugs can snyi po zhes bya bar shes so/gzhon nu gzugs can snyi pos blon po'i bu lnga brgya po dag la smras pa/shes ldan dang /ang ga'i rgyal po 'di ni gdug pa pha rol gnon pa dbud dang mthu 
6b4('dul ba): che bar gyur pa yin pas yu bu cag gis 'di dang lhan cig g.yul sprang par ni mi nus kyis/'di ltar de bag yangs su go phung pa'i phrum brdzi zhing bzhabs te gsad par bya'o//de dag gis de bag yangs su go phud pa'i phrum 
6b5('dul ba): brdzis nas de dag gis ang ga'i rgyal po bzhabs te bsad do//ang ga'i rgyal po'i dpung gi tshogs yan lag bzhi pa phyogs bzhir byer ba dang /gzhon nu gzugs can snyong pos phyogs bzhir pho nya rta pa dag bkye ste/shes ldan dag de 
6b6('dul ba): yang rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi bor dbang bskur ba yin la/nged kyang rgyal po rgyal rigs spyi por dbang bskur ba yin gyis phyir log shig dang /shes ldan dag ngas shes ldan dag la 'tsho ba'i rgyu sbyin par bya'o//zhes spring pa dang de dag 
6b7('dul ba): phyir log go/de nas ang ga'i rgyal pos grong khyer tsam pa bsrung ba bzhag pa des ang ga'i rgyal po ji ltar bsad pa thos nas de sgo bcad der ba dag gi khar sbrengs nas 'dug go/gzhon nu gzugs can snying po yang mthar gyis 
6b8('dul ba): grong khyer tsam par phyin nas des shing ring po la ang ga'i rgyal po'i mgo btags te bstan nas smras pa/ngas khyod kyi jo bo gang yin pa de gnas skabs 'di lta bur byas kyis/gal te je phyir 'byung na de lta na legs/gal te phyir 
7a1('dul ba): mi 'byung na khyod kyang gnas skabs 'di lta bur byas par 'gyur ba gang yin pa de lta bu'i gnas yod par 'gyur ro//des thos nas mi bde bar gyur te/des bsams pa/yul dang srog la gnod gyur na//skyes bus kun du srog 
7a2('dul ba): bsrung bya/blo yis gnyi ga dpyang byas na//yul ni yang rnyed srog rnams min//snyam nas de ral gri mgul du thogs te phyir byung ngo //de na gzhon nu gzugs can snying pos ang ga'i rgyal po bsad de grong khyer tsam pa rang 
7a3('dul ba): gi lag tu thob nas des rgyal po pad ma chen po la lha bdag gis ang ga'i rgyal po bkum ste grong khyer tsam pa rang gi sug par thob lags na/lha yongs su ma grub pa gzhan ci mchis bka' stsol cig dang yongs su bsgrub par bgyi'o 
7a4('dul ba): zhes phrin yig spring ngo //de mgu nas des de la gdugs dang /cod pan dang /thod bskur te/bu khyod kyis 'o ni nyid du rgyal srid byed du chug shig/ngas kyang 'di nyid du rgyal srid byed du gzhug go zhes spring ngo //de nas

7a7('dul ba): po gzugs can snying pos yul ang ga dang ma ga d+ha dag tu rgyal srid 'byor pa/rgyas pa/bde ba/lo legs pa/skye bo dang mi mang pos gdab/thab mo dang /'bab dang phyi dgra dang /nad 'khrug rab tu zhi ba/chom kun dang /mu ge dang /
7a8('dul ba): nad dang bral ba/sa lu dang /bu ram shing gi phrang ba dang /ba lang dang /ma he phun sum tshogs pa byed du bcug ste/chos dang ldan pa chos kyi rgyal pos chos kyi rgyal srid byed du bcug go/de nas bram ze'i khye'u gsang tshig 
7b1('dul ba): 'dod pa gsang tshig tshol ba zhig yul dbus nas lho phyogs su song ngo //de na bram ze rig byed dang rig byed kyi yan lag gi pha rol tu son pa yul tha dad pa na gnas pa'i bram ze rnams kyi ston par gyur 
7b2('dul ba): pa zhig 'dug nas/bram ze'i khye'u de bram ze de'i drung du song ste phyin nas zhi ba dang spyod lam zhi bas mgo bos phyag 'tshal te phyogs gcig tu 'dug go/de nas bram ze des bram ze'i khye'u de la 'ongs pa legs so zhes bya ba'i

7b5('dul ba): tshe mi klog par gyur pa de'i tshe na lan 'ga' ni chu dogs su nye reg byed du 'dong ngo //lan 'ga' ni grong khyer ltar mdong ngo //lan 'ga' ni yam sreg shing thur 'dod do//ji tsam dus gzhan zhig na de dag mi klog par gyur nas de'i tshe de dag 
7b6('dul ba): yam sreg shing thur dod pa dang /de dag lam gyi bar du bar skabs kyi gtam rjod pa 'di lta bu 'di byung ste/kye ku dasa'i bu dag bar sa'i bu dag shan ti la'i bu dag/bar dwa dza'i bu dag/lnga pa'i bu dag/nye lnga'i bu dag/rje khyed cag las gang ni yul ga las 
7b7('dul ba): 'ongs pa dri bar bya'o//de na bram ze'i khye'u gcig gis smras pa/kho bo ni yul shar nas so//gzhan zhig gis smras pa/kho bo ni lho phyogs nas so//yang gzhan zhig gis smras pa//kho bo ni nub phyogs nas so//yang gzhag zhig gis smras 
7b8('dul ba): pa/kho bo ni byang phyogs nas so//bram ze'i khye'u des smras pa/kho bo ni yul dbus nas so//de dag gis smras pa/shes ldan dag bdag cag gis yul thams cad ni thong zhing thos na yul dbus ni ma mthong ma thos so//shar na blo can 
8a1('dul ba): @#//gnas pa ste/lho phyogs na ni g.yon byed 'dug/nub kyi yul na phra ma can//byang phyogs na ni tshig rtsub po//zhes tshigs su gcad pa yang smras so//de nas dris pa/bram ze'i khye'u yul dbus ci 'dra ba/shes ldan dag yul dbus 
8a2('dul ba): ni yul mchog yin te/sa lu dang /bu ram shing gi phreng ba dang /ba lang dang /ma he phun sum tshogs pa/slong mo pa brgyas khyab pa/dbus pa'i skye bos gang ba/mtha' khob pa'i skye bos spangs pa/skye bo mkhas 
8a3('dul ba): pas bsten pa/de na chu klung gang gA kha rje dang /bsod nams dang /bkra shis dang /gtsang ba dang /gtsang ba nyid kyi gnas su grags pa/'gram pa gnyis nas rlan 'phyo pa/drang srong rnams kyi sti gnas shes bya ba chu dgur bcwa

8a6('dul ba): khye'u yul dbus na 'di lta ste dper na bdag cag gi mkhan po dang 'dra ba smra ba'i khyu mchog de lta bu dag yod dam/shes ldan dag yul dbus na bdag cag gi mkhan pas gang dag gi gdod du lta bar yang mi nus pa/gnyan par smra ba/smra 
8a7('dul ba): ba'i khyu mchog de lta bu ga yod do//bram ze'i khye'u des de dag la de lta de ltar yul dbus kyi bsngags pa rjod pa dang /'di ltar de dag thams cad yul dbus su 'dod 'dod par gyur to//de nas bram ze'i khye'u de dag yam sreg shing dag 
8a8('dul ba): btsal te/yam sreg shing gi khur thogs nas bram ze'i khyim ga la ba der dong ste phyin nas yam sreg shing gi khur dag mtha' cig tu bzhag ste bram ze de ga la ba der dong nas phyin pa dang /bram ze de la 'di skad ces smras so//mkhan 
8b1('dul ba): po mkhyen par mdzod cig/bram ze'i khye'u 'dis ci nas kyang bdag cag thams cad yul dbus su mchi 'tshal bar gyur pa de ltar yul dbus kyi bsngags pa brjod do//bram ze des smras pa /bu dag ji snyed
8b2('dul ba): thos pa de snyed bgrod par bya ba yin nam /'di ltar yul dag ni thos na dga' bar 'gyur ba yin pas mnyan par bya ba'i bgrod par ni mi bya'o //mkhan po bram ze'i khye'u 'di'i mchid nas yul dbus na bdag cag gi mkhan pos gang
8b3('dul ba): dag gi gdong du lta bar yang mi nus pa/snyan par smra ba /smra ba'i khyu mchog de lta bu dag mchis so/zhes mchi'o//bu dag ci kho bo na re sa'i steng na kho bo gcig bu kho na smra ba yin gyi /smra ba gzhan 'ga' yang med do /zhes zer
8b4('dul ba): ram;sa ni rin po che mang po 'dzin pa yin pas sa gzhi ni mdzes pa mdzes pa dag gis gang ba yin no //mkhan po re shig yul de yang mthong bar 'gyur zhing chu dogs su nyi reg kyang bgyi /smra ba'i khyu mchog de dag la yang bsnyen
8b5('dul ba): bkur bgyi/rgol ba dag kyang tshar bcad par bgyi/rang gi grags pa yang bsgrag par bgyi/rnyed pa yang bsgrub par bgyis/bdag cag yul dbus su mchi'o //bram ze de slob ma la rjes su chags pa dang bcas pa dang //

10a1('dul ba): @#//rgyal pos blon po rnams la bsgo ba/shes ldan dag rgol ba'i dkyil 'khor shoms shig/phyogs dang gzhan gyi phyogs dag rnam par zhog shig/blon po rnams kyis rgol ba'i dkyil 'khor bshams shing phyogs dang
